\subsection{Model Equation}\label{model-equation}

\[
 \small
\begin{array}{lrl}
  \text{sale\_price} & \;=\; & 3,768,353.90 \\
  & & + {}^{0}g^{1}_{1} + {}^{0}g^{1}_{2} + {}^{0}g^{1}_{3} + {}^{0}g^{1}_{4} \\
  & & + {}^{0}g^{1}_{5} + {}^{0}g^{1}_{6} + {}^{0}g^{1}_{7} + {}^{0}g^{2}_{1} \\
  & & + {}^{0}g^{2}_{2} + {}^{0}g^{2}_{3} \\
  & & \\ \\
  \text{living\_sqft} & {}^{0}g^{1}_{1} \;=\; & -1,625.48 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{living\_sqft} - 2,970.00) \\
  & & \quad - \; 501.8024 \cdot \max(0,\, 2,970.00 - \text{living\_sqft}) \\
  \text{lot\_size} & {}^{0}g^{1}_{2} \;=\; & 341.5122 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{lot\_size} - 5,974.00) \\
  & & \quad - \; 283.1557 \cdot \max(0,\, 5,974.00 - \text{lot\_size}) \\
  & & \quad - \; 116.5573 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{lot\_size} - 8,517.00) \\
  & & \quad - \; 226.3866 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{lot\_size} - 4,702.00) \\
  \text{age} & {}^{0}g^{1}_{3} \;=\; & 18,289.17 \cdot \max(0,\, 39.0000 - \text{age}) \\
  & & \quad - \; 13,066.72 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{age} - 100.0000) \\
  \text{sale\_age} & {}^{0}g^{1}_{4} \;=\; & -3,619.26 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{sale\_age} - 1,393.00) \\
  & & \quad + \; 454.2238 \cdot \max(0,\, 1,393.00 - \text{sale\_age}) \\
  & & \quad + \; 918.0035 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{sale\_age} - 1,979.00) \\
  & & \quad + \; 2,345.29 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{sale\_age} - 1,192.00) \\
  \text{area\_id} & {}^{0}g^{1}_{5} \;=\; & -395,652.72 \cdot I\{\text{area\_id} = \text{471}\} \\
  & & \quad + \; 352,033.08 \cdot I\{\text{area\_id} = \text{460}\} \\
  & & \quad + \; 256,537.40 \cdot I\{\text{area\_id} = \text{466}\} \\
  & & \quad + \; 254,469.04 \cdot I\{\text{area\_id} = \text{463}\} \\
  \text{garage\_spaces} & {}^{0}g^{1}_{6} \;=\; & -369,934.84 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{garage\_spaces} - 2.0000) \\
  \text{baths\_total} & {}^{0}g^{1}_{7} \;=\; & -138,682.66 \cdot \max(0,\, 4.5000 - \text{baths\_total}) \\
  \{\text{living\_sqft},\, \text{sale\_age}\} & {}^{0}g^{2}_{1} \;=\; & -11.3049 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{sale\_age} - 757.0000) \cdot \max(0,\, \text{living\_sqft} - 2,970.00) \\
  & & \quad + \; 2.4826 \cdot \max(0,\, 757.0000 - \text{sale\_age}) \cdot \max(0,\, \text{living\_sqft} - 2,970.00) \\
  & & \quad - \; 1.0225 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{sale\_age} - 1,154.00) \cdot \max(0,\, 2,970.00 - \text{living\_sqft}) \\
  & & \quad + \; 0.974988 \cdot \max(0,\, 1,154.00 - \text{sale\_age}) \cdot \max(0,\, 2,970.00 - \text{living\_sqft}) \\
  & & \quad + \; 11.3282 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{sale\_age} - 552.0000) \cdot \max(0,\, \text{living\_sqft} - 2,970.00) \\
  & & \quad + \; 2.0032 \cdot \max(0,\, 1,393.00 - \text{sale\_age}) \cdot \max(0,\, \text{living\_sqft} - 3,284.00) \\
  & & \quad - \; 0.934539 \cdot \max(0,\, 1,393.00 - \text{sale\_age}) \cdot \max(0,\, 3,284.00 - \text{living\_sqft}) \\
  & & \quad + \; 1.3505 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{sale\_age} - 1,426.00) \cdot \max(0,\, 2,970.00 - \text{living\_sqft}) \\
  \{\text{beds\_total},\, \text{living\_sqft}\} & {}^{0}g^{2}_{2} \;=\; & -176.4139 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{living\_sqft} - 2,970.00) \cdot \max(0,\, \text{beds\_total} - 4.0000) \\
  & & \quad - \; 2,202.79 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{living\_sqft} - 2,970.00) \cdot \max(0,\, 4.0000 - \text{beds\_total}) \\
  \{\text{baths\_total},\, \text{living\_sqft}\} & {}^{0}g^{2}_{3} \;=\; & 273.1670 \cdot \max(0,\, \text{living\_sqft} - 2,970.00) \cdot \max(0,\, \text{baths\_total} - 4.5000)
\end{array} 
\]
